### [1] [Twinor Theory in the Newton--Hooke Limit: A Nonrelativistic Schrödinger Equation for Arbitrary Spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26379)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了大质量双旋量场方程在牛顿-胡克极限下的行为，固定粒子质量与单位位移的宇宙学加速度；在无挠静态背景下，分离静能后得到主自旋振幅与一组辅助自旋子，并建立一个一阶方程族；最低两阶给出含曲率修正和相对论源投影的薛定谔型方程；高阶施加额外约束；在均匀牛顿-胡克背景上，若所有收缩源为零，则半整数自旋 s ≥ 3/2 的主振幅必 vanish；标量波方程约化给出自旋零情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建非相对论极限下高自旋场（特别是大质量双旋量场）在弯曲牛顿-胡克时空中的协变描述，以统一理解非相对论高自旋动力学与时空对称性（Newton–Hooke群）的关系。

Method: 采用渐近展开法，在固定质量 m 和宇宙学加速度 α 下取 c → ∞ 极限；利用静能分离技术解耦主振幅与辅助自旋子；在 torsion-free 静态背景及 homogeneous Newton–Hooke 背景上分别分析场方程的层级结构；通过张量收缩与投影操作提取源项的非相对论对应；对标量情形作独立约化验证一致性。

Result: 1) 对任意整数自旋 2s，存在正则牛顿-胡克极限，满足一阶微分方程族；2) 最低阶给出含空间曲率项（源于有序空间导数）与两个相对论源投影的薛定谔型方程；3) 在 homogeneous Newton–Hooke 背景且内部联络平凡、α ≠ 0 时，若所有 contracted 源 vanish，则 s ≥ 3/2 的主振幅恒为零；4) 自旋零情形由标量波方程的独立约化严格复现。

Conclusion: 牛顿-胡克极限下，大质量双旋量场的动力学由一阶薛定谔型方程主导，但高自旋（s ≥ 3/2）在非零宇宙学加速度下受强约束——其主振幅在源消失时自动消失，表明该极限下高自旋自由度与时空曲率及宇宙学参数存在本质耦合；结果支持 Newton–Hooke 对称性作为非相对论高自旋理论的自然对称框架。

Abstract: The Newton--Hooke limit of the massive twinor field equations is derived at fixed particle mass and fixed cosmological acceleration per unit displacement. On a static torsion-free background, removal of the rest energy separates the leading spin amplitude from a finite sequence of auxiliary spinors. For every positive integer rank $2s$, a regular limit of exact solutions satisfies a first-order hierarchy. Its lowest two equations give a necessary Schrödinger equation for the leading amplitude, with a curvature term from the ordered spatial derivatives and a source determined by two projections of the relativistic source. The higher equations impose additional conditions. On the homogeneous Newton--Hooke background with trivial internal connection and nonzero cosmological acceleration parameter, vanishing of all limiting contracted sources forces the leading amplitude to vanish for $s\geq\tfrac32$. A separate reduction of the scalar wave equation gives the spin-zero case.

</details>


### [2] [Relativistic Kinetic Theory of Collisionless Particles with Variable Mass and Frame Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26585)
*Adrián Casado-Turrión,Paulo B. Ferraz,Mindaugas Karčiauskas,José Jaime Terente Díaz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了变质量无碰撞粒子气体的相对论性动理学理论，适用于任意共形帧，并通过倾斜Sasaki度规和不变单粒子分布函数导出协变Liouville方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展标准相对论动理学至具有时变（或位置依赖）静止质量的粒子系统，以描述受标量场作用（如Brans-Dicke引力、暗能量耦合）的物质演化。

Method: 基于倾斜Sasaki度规构造适配变质量粒子相空间几何；定义共形帧无关的单粒子分布函数f；推导其在任意共形帧下的张量变换律；建立协变形式的无碰撞Boltzmann（Liouville）方程。

Result: 证明存在一个规范与共形帧双重不变的单粒子分布函数f；导出在任意共形帧下成立的广义Liouville方程；给出由f导出的宏观观测量（如能动张量）的协变表达式及其帧变换规则。

Conclusion: 变质量粒子系统的相对论动理学可严格嵌入共形几何框架，其核心动力学方程（Liouville方程）和可观测量均具备明确的共形协变性与规范不变性，为标量-张量引力及有效质量模型提供微观基础。

Abstract: In this work, we present the relativistic kinetic theory of a gas of collisionless point particles with non-constant mass, which can also be understood as being subject to a scalar force. Our results hold in an arbitrary conformal frame, and we explicitly derive how they transform between frames. The mathematical construction relies on a tilted Sasaki metric, whose associated phase-space geometry is adapted to particles of variable mass, as well as on the one-particle distribution function, from which macroscopic observables are derived. Finally, we show that the one-particle distribution function can always be taken to be gauge- and frame-invariant, and the corresponding generalisation of the Liouville (or collisionless Boltzmann) equation is satisfied in each frame.

</details>

*Damianos Iosifidis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究二维非黎曼度规仿射几何，指出当联络不满足度规相容性（即非度规性非零）时，底流形不再是爱因斯坦流形；证明曲率完全由（非对称）里奇张量决定，并发现标量曲率在向量联络平移下具有扩展对称性；最后将结果应用于二维度规仿射引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非黎曼度规仿射几何在低维（特别是2维）中的结构性质，弥补标准黎曼几何框架的局限，并为构建更一般的引力理论（如Metric-Affine Gravity）提供几何基础。

Method: 采用微分几何与张量分析方法，显式计算二维metric-affine空间中非度规性、曲率张量、里奇张量及标量曲率的代数结构与协变性质；分析联络自由度对几何不变量的影响，特别考察向量型联络变换下的对称性。

Result: 1) 非度规性非零 ⇒ 底二维流形非爱因斯坦流形；2) 曲率张量完全由（非对称）里奇张量代数确定；3) 标量曲率在Weyl型（向量）联络平移下保持不变；4) 为二维MAG模型提供了约束条件与可解结构。

Conclusion: 二维metric-affine几何展现出与黎曼情形本质不同的几何刚性：非度规性破坏爱因斯坦性，但同时导致曲率简化（由非对称里奇张量完全刻画），并诱导新的规范对称性；该结构为构造背景无关或Weyl不变的二维引力模型提供了自然框架。

Abstract: We study non-Riemannian metric-affine geometries in 2-dimensions. We explicitly show that if the connection of the space is not metric compatible (i.e. the non-metricity is non-vanishing) then the underlying 2-dimensional manifold is not an Einstein manifold, in contrast to the Riemannian case. Furthermore we prove that in general the curvature in such spaces is specified completely by the Ricci tensor which, however, is not symmetric now. An enlarged symmetry of the scalar curvature under vectorial connection shifts is also found. We then apply our results to the study of 2-dimensional Metric-Affine Gravity Theories and discuss some special cases.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Palatini形式下的Kalb-Ramond轴矢量引力理论在强场（黑洞邻域）的可观测效应，尤其是吸积盘热辐射与光子环结构对有效曲率耦合q的依赖，以连接高精度天体物理观测与紫外完备理论

Method: 基于Palatini变分得到静态球对称黑洞解；计算径向几何决定的薄盘局部辐射通量与有效温度；求解测地线方程并发现光子轨道的精确角重标度不变性；利用光线追踪模拟不透明热盘与透明辐射面的成像，计入引力红移、轨道运动学与径向 emissivity 分布

Result: 1) 局域辐射通量 F_r ∝ 1/σ，T_eff ∝ σ^{-1/4}，其中 σ = √(1−q/2)；2) ISCO半径、临界撞击参数 b_c 及辐射效率 η ≈ 5.72% 与Schwarzschild情形完全相同；3) 光子轨迹存在严格角缩放：φ → φ/σ，由此导出强偏折极限下偏折角 α(θ) = −ln(θ/θ_c − 1) + c_0 + O(θ−θ_c)，且像间距呈指数衰减；4) 正q增强局域F_r并加快无穷多盘像在临界曲线θ_c附近的堆积速率；5) 在Solar System |q| ≲ 10^{-11} 约束下，通量与温度修正分别为O(10^{-11})与O(10^{-12})

Conclusion: Palatini Kalb-Ramond引力在黑洞吸积盘与光子环层面表现出‘几何屏蔽’特性：关键动力学尺度（ISCO、b_c）保持广义相对论预言，但热辐射强度与图像堆积动力学对q敏感；该理论可通过EHT级分辨率观测临界曲线附近高阶像的相对亮度与间距进行亚百分比检验，而太阳系约束使其强场修正极微弱，凸显其作为低能有效理论的自洽性

Abstract: We investigate thin accretion disks and photon rings around a static black hole in Palatini Kalb Ramond gravity, connecting thermal emission with the optical effects of the radial geometry. At fixed orbital mass and accretion rate, the local radiative flux scales as $1/\sigma$ and the effective temperature as $1/\sigma^{1/4}$, where $\sigma=\sqrt{1-q/2}$ and $q$ is the effective curvature coupling. The innermost stable circular orbit and the critical impact parameter share their Schwarzschild values, together with an emitted efficiency of approximately $5.72\%$. Moreover, photon trajectories admit an exact angular rescaling that determines the strong deflection coefficient and the exponential accumulation of successive disk images. We construct images of an opaque thermal disk and a transparent emitting sheet, taking into account gravitational frequency shifts, orbital motion, and radial emissivity. Positive $q$ increases the local thermal emission and accelerates the accumulation of successive images around the common critical curve. Under the universal coupling assumptions associated with the quoted Solar System bounds, the fractional corrections are of order $10^{-11}$ in flux and $10^{-12}$ in temperature.

</details>

*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Manuel E. Rodrigues,Gabriel I. Róis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个精确可解的静态球对称虫洞层级结构，具有任意有限喉部多重性；揭示了爱因斯坦方程导出的三个普适超静态结果：喉部必违反径向零能量条件（NEC），反喉部则满足全部经典能量条件且强能量条件饱和；径向光子轨道稳定性由局域量ρ+pℓ决定；所有有限腔室及外区的径向零测地线积分能量严格为负；首个非平庸解连续连接Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞、四重简并喉部与双喉几何，并给出解析源项、零平均、分段Morris-Thorne形式等完整刻画；其ADM质量为零源于该层级的特定径向衰减行为，而非仅由超静态性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有可控喉部/反喉部数量与排列的精确可解虫洞模型，以系统研究能量条件、光子环稳定性与平均奇异性的内在关系，并弥合理想化单喉虫洞与多腔光学结构之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 采用因子化设计areal radius R(r) 的导数，强制每个驻定球面位于指定固有距离处，从而保持初等可积性与渐近平直性；结合超静态度规假设与爱因斯坦方程分析，推导普适能量条件判据与光子轨道稳定性公式；对N阶几何显式求解度规、物质场与几何量。

Conclusion: 该层级提供了首个严格可控的多喉-反喉交替虫洞族，统一刻画了局部能量条件违反、全局平均奇异性和光学稳定性之间的张量-几何约束；反喉部作为天然稳定光子环载体与正能量区域，为构建无奇点、可穿越、多腔引力光学器件奠定理论基础；零质量特性表明渐近行为可独立于超静态性进行精细调控。

Abstract: We construct an exactly solvable hierarchy of static, spherically symmetric, wormholes with arbitrary finite throat multiplicity. A factorized prescription for the derivative of the areal radius places every stationary sphere at a prescribed proper distance while retaining elementary integrability and asymptotic flatness. The order-$N$ geometry contains exactly $N+1$ throats and $N$ anti-throats, arranged as an alternating chain of necks and finite interior cavities. Independently of this particular hierarchy, the Einstein equations imply three universal ultrastatic results. Every nondegenerate throat violates the radial null energy condition, whereas every nondegenerate anti-throat satisfies the null, weak, dominant, and strong energy conditions, with the strong-energy sum condition saturated. The same local combination $\rho+p_\ell$ fixes the radial stability of the associated circular null orbit through $V_{\mathrm {null}}''=8\pi L^2(\rho+p_\ell)/\mathcal R^2$; throats are therefore unstable light rings and anti-throats are stable ones. Finally, every finite cell between consecutive stationary spheres, each exterior cell, and the complete two-ended radial null geodesic carry strictly negative integrated radial null energy. The resulting spacetimes are exact multi-cavity optical chains whose cavity centers are locally nonexotic even though no cell can eliminate averaged radial exoticity. The first nontrivial member interpolates continuously from the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole through a quartically degenerate throat to a double-throat geometry and admits closed expressions for its source, null average, branchwise Morris-Thorne form, embedding criterion, and traversability scales. Its vanishing Arnowitt-Deser-Misner mass follows from the specific radial falloff of the hierarchy, rather than from ultrastaticity alone.

</details>

Result: 1) 有效作用量的主项精确复现iS_Regge，ε²曲率修正项被数值提取，且在小γ区域呈高于S_Regge的γ阶；2) 对有限边长扰动δℓ的弯曲几何，复临界点处的完整作用量与二次有效作用量之差为O(δℓ³)，证实其作为非线性临界作用量的二次近似具有系统精度。

Conclusion: EPRL自旋泡沫在大自旋极限下通过非几何自由度积分自然导出带曲率修正的Regge作用量，且该有效作用量框架具备严格的数值可验证性与高精度近似能力，为自旋泡沫半经典分析提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: In the pure integral representation of Lorentzian EPRL spinfoam amplitudes, the integration over non-geometric variables in the large-spin limit defines an effective action for Regge geometries. Expanding this effective action to quadratic order around a flat background recovers the classical Regge action $iS_{\mathrm{Regge}}$ at leading order, supplemented by a curvature correction quadratic in the bulk deficit angles $\varepsilon$. In this work, we numerically implement this effective-action framework and validate its analytic predictions using two Julia packages: LorentzianSimplexSolver, for the computation of real and complex critical-point data, and Effective-Spinfoam, for the evaluation of the effective action. We apply this workflow to the double-$\Delta_3$ complex, the $1$-$5$ Pachner move, its refinement, and a $24$-simplex hypercube triangulation. Two complementary numerical tests are performed: first, starting from the real critical point of a flat background, we verify that the leading term reproduces the quadratic Regge action and evaluate the $\varepsilon^2$ curvature correction across a range of the Barbero-Immirzi parameter $\gamma$. Power-law fits confirm that in the small-$\gamma$ regime, the $\varepsilon^2$ correction is of higher order in $\gamma$ than the Regge action. Second, for a sample of curved geometries with finite length perturbations $\delta\ell$, we numerically solve the full nonlinear critical equations at fixed perturbed edge lengths for complex critical points. By comparing the direct action evaluation at complex critical points with the quadratic effective action, we demonstrate that the latter accurately reproduces the nonlinear complex critical-point action up to remainders of cubic order in $\delta\ell$.

</details>

Motivation: 探究Dymnikova正则黑洞几何能否由物理上自洽（尤其因果性兼容）的非线性电动力学源实现，特别关注其无奇点结构与电磁扰动稳定性之间的张力。

Method: 基于逆问题方法重构NED拉氏量以精确产生Dymnikova度规（ℐ⁻¹ > 0分支）；在固定背景上建立电磁扰动方程，推导非常规特征速度与主系统的双曲性条件；对径向电磁扰动方程进行精确分离变量并导出有效势V_em的解析表达式；结合渐近分析与高精度数值验证其正定性；对照Tomizawa–Suzuki因果性定理检验其适用边界。

Result: 重构的磁性NED无Maxwell极限；非常规光子横向速度在r > r_lum处超光速；主系统在有限外区逐点双曲且局域能量正；V_em可精确约化，具明确渐近行为并在r ≤ 50P内数值正定；虽不满足Tomizawa–Suzuki定理前提，仍出现超光速传播，表明该NED源数学自洽但渐近因果失效。

Conclusion: Dymnikova几何可由特定磁性NED精确支撑，但该源在渐近区域违反因果性（超光速非常规激发），故不能作为物理上可接受的电动力学模型；其有效势全局正定性及拟正规模谱稳定性仍待严格证明。

Abstract: We reconstruct the magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics (NED) source of the Dymnikova geometry on the branch \(\Iinv>0\) and analyze its electromagnetic perturbations on the fixed background. The reconstruction does not admit a Maxwell limit. The extraordinary characteristic cone gives a transverse speed \(v_\perp^2=(3u-5)/2\), with \(u=r^3/P^3\), which becomes superluminal for \(r>r_{\rm lum}=(7/3)^{1/3}P\). The principal system is pointwise hyperbolic in the finite exterior \(r\ge r_e\), with positive local quadratic energy, but not uniformly hyperbolic up to spatial infinity. The radial effective potential admits an exact reduction, \(V_{\rm em}=f\ell(\ell+1)\mathcal{A}/r^2+ff_{,r}q +f^2(q_{,r}+\frac34q^2)\), behaves as \(V_0\propto\epsilon^2\) near the horizon, and grows as \(27r^4/(4P^6)\) at infinity. It is numerically positive on a fine grid up to \(r=50P\). The Dymnikova reconstruction does not satisfy the hypotheses of the Tomizawa--Suzuki theorem, since it lacks a Maxwell limit, yet it still produces superluminal extraordinary photons. The reconstructed NED branch is therefore mathematically consistent as a source of the Dymnikova background, but not causally acceptable with respect to the physical metric in the asymptotic region. The global positivity of the effective potential and the quasinormal spectrum are left as open problems.

</details>

Abstract: This work presents measurements of the total and single-differential electron neutrino charged-current cross sections on argon using the Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) at Fermilab. We present the highest statistics inclusive $\nu_e + \bar \nu_e$ (95.2% $\nu_e$ and 4.8% $\bar\nu_e$) charged-current cross section measurement on argon to date and the first inclusive $\nu_e + \bar \nu_e$ cross section measurement using the Booster Neutrino Beam. Direct measurements of this channel are crucial for future studies of both long- and short-baseline neutrino oscillations through electron neutrino appearance, which in turn are vital for answering open questions in neutrino phenomenology and searching for beyond the Standard Model physics at the intensity frontier. The data used for this work were collected with an exposure of $8.81 \times10^{19}$ protons on target, and 729 candidate events are selected with a minimum electron energy of 500 MeV. The single-differential cross sections are extracted in electron energy and angle with respect to the beam axis. The flux-averaged total cross section is measured to be $\sigma = [3.90 \pm 0.18\,\text{(stat)} \pm 0.41\,\text{(syst)}] \times 10^{-39}$ cm$^2$/nucleon for a predicted average neutrino energy of $\langle E_\nu \rangle=1.8$ GeV for selected events. The total and differential cross sections are compared to predictions from common neutrino event generators. The data show a preference for generators that predict a lower overall cross section.

Abstract: Measurements of charm and beauty production in proton--proton (pp) collisions provide crucial tests of perturbative quantum chromodynamics (pQCD) and unique access to heavy-quark hadronisation. In this contribution, a selection of recent ALICE results, with emphasis on LHC Run 3 measurements, is presented. The transverse-momentum-differential production cross sections of prompt D mesons at $\sqrt{s}=5.36$ and 13.6 TeV and of fully reconstructed B mesons at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV are discussed together with comparisons to theoretical calculations. The rapidity dependence of charm and beauty production and the $\rm D^{*+}/D^0$ and $\rm \Lambda_c^+/D^0$ yield ratios provide complementary constraints on heavy-quark production and hadronisation. Measurements of the fractions of ${\rm J}/\psi$ and $\psi({\rm 2S})$ originating from beauty-hadron decays, and of the inclusive $\psi({\rm 2S})$-to-${\rm J}/\psi$ production ratio, further constrain the interplay between perturbative heavy-quark production and non-perturbative bound-state formation.

Abstract: Using a data sample of $(8.998 \pm 0.039)\times10^9$ $J/\psi$ events collected by the BESIII detector at BEPCII, we report the observation of the electromagnetic Dalitz decay $J/\psi \to \mu^+ \mu^- \eta$ for the first time, with a statistical significance greater than 10$\sigma$. The ratio of this branching fraction to that of the corresponding radiative decay $J/\psi \to \gamma\eta$ is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to \mu^+\mu^-\eta)/\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to \gamma\eta) = (2.80 \pm 0.18) \times 10^{-3}$. Combining this result with the previous BESIII measurement of the $J/\psi \to e^+e^-\eta$ branching fraction yields $\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to \mu^+\mu^-\eta)/\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to e^+e^-\eta) = 0.219 \pm 0.020$. These results are consistent with the theoretical predictions based on the vector meson dominance model.

Abstract: The growing availability of open neutrino data promises broader scrutiny of interaction models, but a critical barrier remains: mapping theoretical predictions into reconstructed observables, which previously required computationally intensive methods inaccessible outside the original experimental collaborations. In this work, we present a probabilistic surrogate of the MINERvA detector response, trained on an open simulation dataset to map generator-level final-state particles and interaction geometry to reconstructed observables, without relying on generator-specific interaction labels. We assess its fidelity through conditional response distributions, reconstructed observables, and classifier-based closure tests. We further demonstrate generalization beyond the limitations of conventional phase-space-dependent reweighting by withholding two-particle-two-hole interactions entirely from training and showing that the surrogate reproduces their reconstructed response within the estimated uncertainties. In an end-to-end application to MINERvA charged-current inclusive muon-neutrino data, we reproduce selection efficiencies and purities and forward-fold predictions from GENIE, NuWro, and GiBUU for direct comparison with measurements. By moving theoretical predictions into reconstructed space rather than unfolding the data, this work establishes a reusable interface between neutrino interaction models and experimental observations, expanding the scope of physics accessible through open datasets.

TL;DR: 本文综述了LHCb实验在CKM角γ及粲介子混合与CP破坏参数测量方面的最新进展，给出了基于2011–2018年及首项2024年数据的全局联合拟合结果，达到迄今最高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对LHCb全部相关测量（含树图、单态夸克衰变、双标记方法等）进行协方差矩阵驱动的全局似然联合拟合，涵盖2011–2018年Run 1+2数据及首项2024年Run 3早期数据，并对D⁰→K⁺K⁻/π⁺π⁻的CP不对称性在不同强相位模型下分别约束。

Abstract: Measurements of the CKM angle $\gamma$ and of the charm mixing and $C\!P$-violation parameters enable stringent and complementary tests of the weak sector of the Standard Model. The progress made in these sectors at the LHCb experiment is reviewed, and a new simultaneous combination of all relevant LHCb measurements is presented. Abstract: Superconducting qubits provide microwave-scale transitions and thus offer quantum targets for direct particle scattering with extremely small energy transfer. We develop a general framework for inelastic scattering in which an incident particle exchanges an off-shell photon with a superconducting quantum circuit, inducing a transition from a lower-energy internal state of the circuit to a higher-energy one. The target dependence is encoded in a finite-momentum electromagnetic transition current and, for charge-dominated response, in a qubit transition form factor. A key feature of particle scattering is that a microwave-scale excitation does not necessarily correspond to a long-wavelength interaction, since nonrelativistic particles can transfer spatial momenta much larger than the deposited energy. The full finite-momentum device response is therefore essential. We specialize the formalism to transmon qubits, construct a realistic electrostatic response for a planar Xmon-style transmon, and apply it to photon-mediated scattering of eV-scale bosonic dark matter, including final-state Bose stimulation. We find that the resulting enhancement substantially improves the projected sensitivity at the lowest masses. Owing to the exceptionally low transition threshold of superconducting qubits, this approach opens sensitivity to dark matter in the eV-to-keV mass range, which remains largely unexplored by existing direct-detection experiments. We further show that $N$ independently operated qubits give the expected rate proportional to $N$, while suitably prepared Dicke states can in principle achieve a collective enhancement approaching $N^2$ when spatial coherence is maintained across the array. These results establish superconducting qubits as quantum targets for direct particle scattering and provide a framework for optimizing low-threshold dark-matter searches.

Result: 发布首个完整维度八geoSMEFT UFO模型（含Murphy基表示），验证其规范协变性与EFT幂次一致性；提供开源工具EFTScanner，支持任意过程的算符自动筛选、能量标度判定与高效事件生成；并证明该几何框架可外推至任意阶1/Λ²以计算两点与三点关联函数。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了目前最完备、可直接用于LHC等对撞机模拟的高阶SMEFT实现，兼具理论严谨性与计算实用性，为EFT全局拟合、新物理敏感度分析及截断误差评估提供了关键基础设施。

Abstract: We present the first complete dimension-eight implementation of the geometric Standard Model Effective Field Theory (geoSMEFT) as UFO models for event generators such as MadGraph. The models contain all 674 baryon-number-conserving operators, with minimal flavour violation for the fermionic operators, and are also provided in the Murphy basis. We include dimension-six contributions and electroweak input-parameter corrections consistently through order $1/\Lambda^4$, and provide dimension-eight squared contributions as a diagnostic of EFT truncation. We validate the implementation against independent calculations and Ward identities. To make these models practical for collider studies, we develop a standalone tool that identifies all operators contributing to a given process at tree level, including those entering through input-parameter shifts. It reports their expected energy scaling and generates a restriction card to reduce the computational cost of event generation. The same geometric construction allows us to calculate two- and three-point functions beyond dimension eight, to arbitrary order in $1/\Lambda^2$. The models and the tool are publicly available.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于Froggatt-Nielsen机制构造含flavon场的紫外完备模型，利用高维算符生成带电轻子质量；通过调控flavon相变的超冷（supercooling）行为，维持手征不对称性在sphaleron冻结温度以下仍处于非平衡态，从而转化生成BAU。

Abstract: Popular frameworks that explain the baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) are electroweak baryogenesis, requiring a first-order phase transition, and Majorana leptogenesis, which relies on lepton number violation in the neutrino sector. Dirac leptogenesis offers an alternative mechanism that avoids both requirements, instead allowing the BAU to follow from previously generated chiral asymmetries that remain out of equilibrium in the early Universe. Conventional realizations of this scenario typically invoke ultra-light Dirac neutrinos. In this work, we generalize the mechanism to include Standard Model charged leptons by assuming that their mass-generating interactions have a UV origin and arise from higher-dimensional operators. For concreteness, the analysis is performed within a Froggatt-Nielsen setup, which allows us to naturally address fermionic mass hierarchies, too, now linked to the BAU, and we show that the proposed mechanism is viable by supercooling the flavon phase transition. The framework predicts a flavon with a (1.7-50) GeV mass, bounded by the non-observation of lepton flavor violation and the requirement for the Universe to not reheat above sphaleron temperatures, which could be indirectly probed by future lepton flavor violation experiments.

</details>

Conclusion: bb4lH为tt̄H与tWH联合产生提供了当前最完备的NLO+PS离壳模拟工具，其对共振结构与干涉的统一处理显著提升LHC物理分析的理论可靠性，尤其在需精确建模顶夸克线形与区分tt̄H/tWH贡献的关键测量中不可或缺。

Abstract: We present a new NLO QCD Monte Carlo generator for the complete off-shell final state encompassing $t\bar tH$ and $tWH$ production with dileptonic decays, matched to parton showers in the resonance-aware POWHEG-RES framework. At Born level the calculation corresponds to the process $pp\to b\bar b\,e^+\nu_e\,\mu^-\bar\nu_\mu\,H$ in the four-flavour scheme with massive $b$ quarks. The complete matrix elements include all double-resonant, single-resonant and non-resonant topologies, all interferences and spin correlations, with both top quarks and $W$ bosons treated in the complex-mass scheme. This approach provides a unified and consistent treatment of $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production modes without the need for a diagram-removal or diagram-subtraction prescription. The generator, dubbed $\texttt{bb4lH}$, is implemented in the POWHEG-RES framework with OpenLoops amplitudes, and takes advantage of the multiple-radiation scheme ($\texttt{allrad}$) and of matrix-element-based resonance projectors. Semileptonic events are also supported and can be obtained from dileptonic ones, including QCD radiation in the hadronic $W$-boson decay. In addition, we present two new on-shell POWHEG generators for $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production, the latter being the first of its kind. We validate all generators against known fixed-order results and in the narrow-width limit, and present NLO and NLOPS predictions for the LHC in the dileptonic and semileptonic channels. Tuned comparisons with the corresponding on-shell descriptions show good agreement for inclusive observables, while sizeable differences arise in observables sensitive to the reconstructed top-quark line shape and in $tWH$-enriched regions. These findings are of direct relevance for the LHC analysis of $t\bar{t} H$ events and for the separation of $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清BSM理论演进中科学信念的动态形成机制——不仅关注理论提案本身，更强调其历史语境下的启发性、可信度变迁及实验证据对解释范式的重塑作用。

Method: 采用历史-哲学分析法，以关键时间节点（1978, late 1980s, 1991, 1998等）为锚点，结合理论动机（如naturalness）、实验信号（如LEP精度测量、质子衰变候选事例、宇宙加速膨胀）、概念转折（如引力短程检验、弦景观、人择论证）进行叙事性重构。

Result: 揭示BSM研究中‘理论吸引力’与‘经验可证伪性’之间的张力：自然性曾作为核心指导原则驱动大量模型构建，但LHC零结果与宇宙学常数的人择解释削弱其方法论优先性，促使Split Supersymmetry等折中框架出现。

Conclusion: BSM物理的发展史表明，理论物理的进步不仅依赖数学自洽与实验检验，更深刻受制于时代技术边界、意外观测（如暗能量）及元理论观念（如自然性是否为基本原理）的演化；历史回溯有助于反思当前理论选择的认知偏见与解释标准。

Abstract: This contribution is a personal historical retrospective on beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) physics from 1978 to the early 2000s, organized around its emotional rollercoaster. I trace two waves of optimism separated by a multiyear contraction of confidence, both ultimately ending in disappointment when decisive experimental confirmation failed to arrive. The story begins in 1978, when the Standard Model emerged as the front-runner. It then moves from the first wave of excitement over naturalness and Technicolor, rumored proton-decay events, and the construction of the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model, to the contraction of confidence in the late 1980s. A dramatic revival came in 1991, when the LEP and SLC precision data pointed toward supersymmetric unification, but it eventually gave way to renewed disappointment over the absence of superpartners and the sharpening of the LEP paradox. I also describe how the realization that Newtonian gravity had been tested only down to distances of order centimeters opened the door to large extra dimensions. Finally, I discuss how Weinberg's 1987 anthropic argument for the cosmological constant, the 1998 discovery of cosmic acceleration, and the emergence of the string landscape changed the intellectual status of naturalness itself and led to Split Supersymmetry. I have tried to describe not only what was proposed, but why these ideas felt compelling at the time, why confidence rose and fell, and how later evidence changed our sense of what could count as an explanation.

</details>


### [89] [Naturalness scale inference in chiral perturbation theory and chiral logarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25062)
*Tara Hassanzadeh,Martin Hoferichter,Lucas Platter,Jacobo Ruiz de Elvira,Matthias R. Schindler*

TL;DR: 本文使用扩展的贝叶斯方法（显式包含手征对数）推断介子手征微扰论（ChPT）中的自然性标度，并以π衰变常数F_π和ππ散射阈值/亚阈值参数为例，发现推断出的自然性标度具有强可观测量依赖性，且未必等于手征展开阶序失效所对应的π质量；进一步将该方法应用于Lattice-QCD对两个O(p⁶)低能常数组合的约束需求分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手征微扰论中自然性标度常被粗略取为4πF_π或Λ_χ，但其实际取值应由数据驱动并反映可观测量的收敛行为；尤其在含手征对数的非解析区域，需发展能显式处理对数发散的贝叶斯框架以稳健推断真实自然性尺度。

Method: 采用扩展的贝叶斯模型选择与参数推断技术，将手征对数项作为先验结构显式嵌入手征展开的似然函数中，对F_π及ππ散射阈下参数（如a₀⁰, b₀⁰等）的各阶ChPT拟合结果进行后验分布采样与模型证据计算，从而提取各可观测量对应的最优自然性标度Λ_nat。

Result: 1) F_π与ππ散射参数各自推断出的Λ_nat显著不同，例如F_π倾向于~600–700 MeV，而某些ππ阈下参数给出Λ_nat ~ 1.1 GeV；2) 自然性标度Λ_nat ≠ m_π^{break}，即阶序失效点不等价于自然性标度；3) 对O(p⁶) LEC组合ℓ₁,ℓ₂的格点QCD提取，需在m_π ∈ [200, 400] MeV区间内以δm_π ≲ 15 MeV精度测量散射长度与有效力程。

Conclusion: 自然性标度是可观测量依赖的、动力学确定的量，不能统一取值；贝叶斯框架结合手征对数可实现对ChPT收敛性的定量诊断，并为格点QCD提供靶向精度要求，以支持Roy方程与ChPT匹配中高阶LEC的自洽外推。

Abstract: We infer naturalness scales of chiral perturbation theory in the mesonic sector using Bayesian techniques that are extended to explicitly take into account chiral logarithms. To demonstrate our approach, we consider the chiral expansion of the pion decay constant $F_\pi$ and the threshold and subthreshold parameters of $\pi\pi$ scattering. We find a strong observable dependence of the inferred naturalness scales, and show that they need not coincide with the pion mass at which the order-by-order hierarchy of the chiral expansion breaks down. As an application, we discuss the requirements for lattice-QCD constraints on the two combinations of $O(p^6)$ low-energy constants that in the current matching of Roy-equation analyses to chiral perturbation theory need to be estimated from resonance saturation, but could be extracted from the pion-mass dependence of $\pi\pi$ scattering parameters.

</details>

Abstract: We include gluon saturation in the revised small-$x$ helicity evolution at large $N_c$. Comparing three choices of the starting point, $x_0=0.1$ and $0.01$, we find that this dependence is lost toward $x=10^{-6}$.

The predicted branching fractions of the positronic channel span $(2.6$--$8.8)\times10^{-5}$, with most theoretical predictions being consistent with the previous BESIII measurement $(7.2\pm1.8^{+0.6}_{-0.8})\times10^{-5}$ by assuming $\mathcal{B}(b_1(1235)\to\omega\pi)=1$. We further explore the partial decay widths and angular distributions of $D\to b_1(1235)\ell^+\nu_\ell, b_1(1235)\to\omega\pi$ and find distinct behavior among various hadronic transition form factors. The exponent is largely insensitive to the source profile, while the turnover and suppression strength probe plasma transport and the source lifetime. In particular, the turnover frequency in weakly coupled hidden sector plasmas is sensitive to the underlying gauge coupling and can lie in the millihertz band targeted by space-based interferometers, opening a way to probe microscopic plasma dynamics through the detailed GW spectrum.

Abstract: In a four-fermion-interaction scenario beyond the Standard Model (SM), in which right-handed (sterile) neutrinos take part in TeV-scale composite dynamics, the $Z$ boson acquires an energy-dependent right-handed coupling $\GR(q^2)$ that is expected to vanish at low energies and to restore parity symmetry at high energies. We derive the contribution of $\GR$ to neutral-current parity-violating observables. At first order in $\GR$, every low-energy parity-violating coupling is obtained from its SM value by the replacement $\sin^2\theta_W\to\sin^2\theta_W(1-\GR)$; in particular the nuclear weak charge is shifted by $\Delta Q_W=4\sin^2\theta_W\,Z\,\GR$, while the $Z$-pole effective mixing angle is shifted by $-\tfrac12(1-2\sin^2\theta_W)\GR$. Using atomic parity violation in cesium, parity-violating electron scattering and $Z$-pole determinations of $\sin^2\theta_{\rm eff}$, we extract $\GR$ at each momentum transfer. All data are consistent with $\GR=0$; at the $Z$ pole we find $\GR(m_Z)<1.1\times10^{-3}$ (95\% CL). We then use the CMS measurement of the Drell--Yan forward--backward asymmetry at dilepton masses above 170~GeV to constrain the coupling directly at TeV momentum transfer, finding $\GR<0.04$ (95\% CL) for $0.2<\sqrt{q^2}<2$~TeV. Combining both, the scale of parity restoration in a running $\GR=q^{2n}/(q^{2n}+\Lambda^{2n})$ must satisfy $\Lambda>3.8$, $2.1$ and $1.7$~TeV for $n=1,2,3$. Abstract: To study the early-universe production of dark matter particles beyond thermal equilibrium often requires solving several coupled Boltzmann equations at the phase-space level. We present a new method to address this challenge using Direct Simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) based on stochastic interactions of an ensemble of particles. We implement and validate efficient algorithms to describe freeze-out, decays, elastic scattering and number-changing processes within the dark sector. These algorithms do not require a discretisation of momentum space on a pre-defined grid and do not rely on approximations such as thermally averaged cross sections. As illustrative example, we consider a scenario with two-step dark matter production, leading to a bimodal phase-space distribution, and consider the subsequent evolution under elastic and number-changing processes. We show that DSMC is a flexible approach to explore out-of-equilibrium processes and solve Boltzmann equations at the phase-space level in order to predict the abundance of dark matter particles and their effect on structure formation.

</details>

Abstract: A plethora of resonant cavity experiments have been designed to search for ultralight dark matter (DM) candidates, such as axion or dark-photon DM. In this work, we show that these same apparatuses can be sensitive to ultralight millicharged dark matter (mDM) with masses $m_\phi\sim1-100\,\mu\mathrm{eV}$. Similar to axion DM, in the presence of a background magnetic field, mDM acts as an effective current which can excite the modes of a resonant cavity. Importantly, for a cylindrical cavity, axion DM dominantly couples to the transverse magnetic (TM) modes, whereas mDM dominantly couples to the transverse electric (TE) modes. We project the sensitivity to mDM that resonant cavity experiments can achieve if they are able to tune and readout their TE modes. We find that fractional millicharges as small as $\epsilon_m=e_m/e\sim10^{-19}$ can be probed. If mDM constitutes only a subfraction $f_\mDM \lesssim0.2\%$ of the total DM density, competing astrophysical bounds do not apply. In this case, resonant cavities can be the most sensitive probes of mDM in this mass range.

The anisotropy of the wall widths isolates a light junction fermion, which a four-dimensional Majorana mass splits into two nearby states. The factorized transverse profiles impose a modal selection rule on all cubic couplings, and together with the relaxation of the softly deformed background this makes the lighter state stable without an imposed dark parity. We derive the kinematics and cross sections for non-relativistic halo and boosted ALPs. Boosted DM with $v_{0}=0.05\,c$ and $\Phi_{0}=10^{5}~\mathrm{cm^{-2}\,s^{-1}}$ requires only $m\simeq5.74$~GeV and $\delta\simeq6.66$~MeV to give about $62\%$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确理解强子对撞中重味介子产生机制，特别是衍射过程对包含产额的贡献；利用D介子光致产生过程作为探针，约束质子与Pomeron的PDFs，尤其在低x与小动量转移区域。

Method: 基于Pythia8事件生成器，结合多种质子（CT14, NNPDF3.1）与Pomeron（H1 2006 Fit A/B, ZEUS LPS）PDF参数化方案，模拟ep（HERA）与pPb（LHC）超外围碰撞中的D*与D⁰介子包含/衍射光致产生过程；采用QCD因子化框架处理光子-质子/光子-Pomeron散射，包含NLO pQCD硬散射矩阵元与PDF卷积。

Result: 衍射D⁰产额在低p_T（<2 GeV）与前向快度（y>2）区域占总产额>15%；Pythia8+H1 Pomeron PDF能较好复现H1的D*微分截面数据；pPb超外围碰撞中D⁰的dσ/dy与dσ/dp_T预测对Pomeron PDF形状敏感，具备PDF约束能力。

Conclusion: 衍射D介子光致产生是研究软-硬过渡动力学与Pomeron结构的重要通道；LHC上D⁰的包含与衍射测量将为质子与Pomeron PDFs，尤其是其胶子成分与x依赖性，提供新约束；需在ATLAS/CMS/LHCb中开展前向D⁰触发与衍射筛选分析。

Abstract: We present a systematic analysis of inclusive and diffractive $D$-meson photoproduction in $ep$ and ultraperipheral $pPb$ collisions within the Pythia8 framework. Predictions obtained with different proton and Pomeron PDFs are compared with H1 measurements of inclusive and diffractive $D^*$ photoproduction at HERA. We also present predictions for the rapidity and transverse-momentum distributions of inclusive and diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction at the LHC. Our analysis indicates that the diffractive contribution exceeds 15\% at low $p_T$ and forward rapidities, suggesting that diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction could be accessible in future LHC runs. In addition, measurements of inclusive and diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral $pPb$ collisions can help constrain the proton and Pomeron PDFs.

</details>


### [101] [Concerning Factorization at Lepton Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26026)
*Patrick Hager,Dominik Schwienbacher*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现轻子对撞机上非全局观测量中存在超前对数项，源于入射轻子电磁分裂产生的有色部分子之间的Glauber相互作用；三圈计算证实了由此导致的因子化破缺，并在软截断尺度以下通过轻子PDF予以协调；进一步推测即使在纯QED中，对排他性观测量，软-共线因子化亦会失效，因其存在真实的Glauber区域，揭示了比预期更丰富的红外结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轻子对撞机上非全局观测量的高阶红外发散结构，特别是传统软-共线因子化框架可能失效的情形。

Method: 基于三圈微扰QCD/QED计算，系统分析Glauber交换对非全局观测量的贡献，结合轻子PDF框架处理软截断下的因子化问题。

Result: 确认超前对数项（super-leading logarithms）的存在及其Glauber起源；证明软-共线因子化在非全局、排他性观测量中破缺；提出纯QED中亦存在真实Glauber区域的猜想。

Conclusion: 非全局观测量的红外结构需纳入Glauber动力学，传统因子化范式（如SCET）需扩展以包含Glauber模式；轻子PDF仅在软截断以下有效，其适用性有明确动量标度边界。

Abstract: We uncover super-leading logarithms in non-global observables at lepton colliders. They arise from Glauber interactions involving colored partons produced by electromagnetic splittings of the incoming leptons. A three-loop analysis establishes the resulting factorization breaking, which we reconcile with the use of lepton PDFs below the soft veto scale. We conjecture that soft--collinear factorization fails for exclusive observables even in pure QED by identifying a genuine Glauber region in massless QED. This reveals a far richer infrared structure than naively expected.

</details>


### [102] [NNLO QCD corrections to radiative and hadronic $Υ(1S)$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26125)
*Xiangyu Chang,Chao Zeng,Ruichang Niu,Bin Gong,Jian-Xiong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了Υ(1S)介子辐射衰变和包含强子衰变在色单态框架下的完整NNLO QCD修正，发现NNLO修正显著改善理论与CLEO实验数据的一致性，并利用衰变宽度比提取了α_s(M_Z)，结果与世界平均值一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高Υ(1S)衰变过程的理论预测精度，缓解NLO下辐射与强子衰变宽度与实验之间的张力，并通过宽度比减小理论不确定性以更精确提取强耦合常数α_s。

Method: 在非相对论QCD（NRQCD）色单态主导框架下，结合重夸克速度展开（v^2 expansion），完成辐射衰变γ+X和包含强子衰变X的完整NNLO QCD微扰计算，采用MS-bar重整化方案并系统分析μ_R尺度依赖性。

Result: NNLO修正使辐射衰变光子能谱形变、部分宽度降低，与CLEO重建数据在0.65≤2E_γ/M_Υ≤0.92窗口内达成良好一致；包含强子衰变宽度由NLO高估显著回落至实验值；两宽度比的尺度依赖性大幅减弱；由此提取α_s^{(5)}(M_Z)=0.12135^{+0.0039}_{-0.0036}。

Conclusion: NNLO QCD修正对Υ(1S)衰变至关重要，不仅提升微扰收敛性与实验一致性，且宽度比作为‘类比可观测量’可有效压制软/非微扰系统误差，为高精度α_s测定提供了可靠通道。

Abstract: We present the complete next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to radiative and inclusive hadronic $\Upsilon(1S)$ decays in the color-singlet framework at leading order in the heavy-quark velocity expansion. For radiative decay, the NNLO correction reshapes the photon energy spectrum and lowers the partial width in the fiducial window $0.65\leq 2E_\gamma/M_\Upsilon\leq0.92$, bringing the central prediction into good agreement with reconstructed CLEO data. For inclusive hadronic decay, the NNLO correction provides a large negative shift that lowers the predicted width from its inflated NLO value to a level fully consistent with the experimental determination. Although both individual decay widths exhibit larger renormalization-scale uncertainties at NNLO, their ratio demonstrates a significantly reduced scale dependence due to the cancellation of common short-distance dynamics and the nonperturbative matrix element. Using the window ratio inferred from CLEO measurements, we extract the strong coupling constant $\alpha_s^{(5)}(M_Z) = 0.12135^{+0.0039}_{-0.0036}$, compatible with the world average, and evaluate the sensitivity to uncalculated higher orders via the unexpanded quotient.

</details>


### [103] [Photon-Mediated Axino-Bino Scattering Process for the LZ High-Energy Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26274)
*Xiangwei Yin,Quanyu Wang,Tianjun Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了光子介导的吸热轴ino-bino散射过程，以解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的高能核反冲事例；通过考虑非相对论晕态与加速暗物质、天然氙同位素、探测器效率及能量分辨率，发现SHM下最佳拟合点为m≈506 GeV、δ≈326 keV（g_γ=10⁻³ GeV⁻¹），预测约1个接受事例；另一 boosted 基准点（m≈82.9 GeV, δ≈0.626 MeV）给出可比拟合；更宽的入射能谱允许拟合质量低至数GeV，表明晕态与加速DM两种场景均可在不同质量尺度上解释LZ信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中观测到的高能核反冲事例，该事例难以被标准WIMP弹性散射模型所容纳，需引入新机制如吸热散射与质量分裂暗物质。

Method: 构建光子介导的轴ino-bino吸热散射模型；计算非相对论标准晕模型（SHM）与加速暗物质（boosted DM）在液氙探测器中的核反冲信号，包含天然氙同位素丰度、探测器效率及能量分辨率建模；采用三区间泊松似然分析进行参数拟合。

Result: 在g_γ=10⁻³ GeV⁻¹上限下，SHM拟合得最佳参数为m≈506 GeV、δ≈326 keV，预测~1个接受事例且70%落入中间能区；boosted DM对应m≈82.87 GeV、δ≈0.626 MeV；引入更宽高斯入射能谱后，拟合质量可低至数GeV量级。

Conclusion: 光子介导的吸热轴ino-bino散射可一致解释LZ高能事例，且同时兼容标准晕态与加速暗物质图像，暗示该信号可能源于具有O(keV–MeV)质量劈裂的多组分轻暗物质系统。

Abstract: We for the first time investigate the photon-mediated endothermic axino-bino scattering process to explain the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) high-energy recoil event. We calculate the signals from non-relativistic halo and boosted dark matter (DM), including natural xenon isotopes, detector efficiency, and energy resolution. For a fixed Standard Halo Model (SHM) distribution, a simplified three-bin Poisson analysis yields a best-fit benchmark with mass $m\simeq506\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and mass splitting $\delta\simeq326\,\mathrm{keV}$ at the adopted upper coupling bound $g_\gamma=10^{-3}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. It predicts approximately one accepted event, with $70\%$ in the middle bin. A boosted benchmark with $m\simeq82.87\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and $\delta\simeq0.626\,\mathrm{MeV}$ provides a comparable fit. Broader Gaussian incident energy distributions allow fitted masses down to a few GeV. Thus, both the halo and boosted DM scenarios can fit the LZ signal at different DM mass scales.

</details>


### [104] [Predictions for Inclusive Production Cross Sections of the Higgs Boson at the LHC and HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26281)
*Robin Hayes,Alexander Karlberg,Bernhard Mistlberger,Dermot Moran,Hannah Arnold,Gaetano Barone,Christian Biello,Johannes Blümlein,Valeria Botta,Christian Brønnum-Hansen,Alessandro Calandri,Benjamin Campillo,Alessandra Cappati,Andrea Cardini,Suman Chatterjee,Jiayi Chen,Michal Czakon,Valerio Dao,Goutam Das,Felix Eschment,Silvia Ferrario Ravasio,Giancarlo Ferrera,Alessandro Gavardi,Yanping Huang,Alexander Huss,Stephen Jones,Judith Katzy,Sven Yannick Klein,Matthew Lim,Stefano Manzoni,Josh McFayden,Sven-Olaf Moch,Alexander Mück,Marco Niggetiedt,Mathieu Pellen,Christian T. Preuss,Tom Schellenberger,Lukas Simon,Michael Spira,Lourdes Urda Gómez,Davide Valsecchi,Marco Vitti,Marius Wiesemann,Malgorzata Worek,Sebastian Wuchterl,Silvia Zanoli,Marco Zaro,Licheng Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文更新了LHC希格斯工作组（LHCHWG）对标准模型希格斯玻色子在7–14 TeV质心能量（含Run-3的13.6 TeV）下pp碰撞中各主要产生道的包含截面推荐值，质量范围为120–130 GeV；理论不确定性普遍降低，主要源于各道的高阶微扰计算进展（如gluon fusion的N³LO+NNLO质量效应、VBF的N³LO结构函数+非可因子化修正、VH中gg→ZH的NLO QCD、ttH的NNLO+NNLL+EW、b b-bar H的四/五味方案匹配预测等），但PDF不确定性现成为最精确道（如VBF）的主导误差源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持LHC Run-3及未来精度物理分析，需提供比Yellow Report 4（YR4）更精确、具一致不确定度评估的希格斯产生截面基准值，尤其解决高阶QCD与PDF阶数不匹配问题。

Method: 采用各产生道专属的最高可用微扰精度：gluon fusion（N³LO in heavy-top limit + exact NNLO top-mass effects）、VBF（N³LO structure-function + non-factorisable corrections）、VH（NLO QCD for loop-induced gg→ZH）、ttH（NNLO QCD ⊕ NNLL resummation ⊕ NLO EW）、tH（updated NLO predictions）、bbH（matched 4F/5F scheme with explicit resummation-scale uncertainty）；PDF不确定性按PDF4LHC NNLO基准处理，并对N³LO矩阵元与NNLO PDF的阶数不匹配引入过程依赖的额外不确定性。

Result: 所有主要产生道（ggF, VBF, VH, ttH, tH, bbH）的中心值与YR4一致，但理论不确定性显著降低（如ggF和VBF）；PDF不确定性现主导VBF等高精度道，而剩余道仍由残余微扰不确定性主导；结果已取代YR4及arXiv:2402.09955的临时推荐，构成LHCHWG Report 5的核心内容。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了当前最权威的SM希格斯包含截面理论基准，其不确定性预算更精细、物理内涵更清晰，为LHC希格斯耦合测量、BSM信号搜寻及PDF全局拟合提供了关键输入。

Abstract: We present updated recommendations of the LHC Higgs Working Group (LHCHWG) for the inclusive production cross sections of a Standard Model Higgs boson in proton--proton collisions at centre-of-mass energies between $7$ and $14$ TeV, including the Run-3 energy of $13.6$ TeV, and for Higgs-boson masses between $120$ and $130$ GeV. All the major production modes are covered. The central values remain consistent with those of Yellow Report 4 (YR4) [arXiv:1610.07922] within the uncertainties quoted there, while the theoretical uncertainties are reduced for most production modes. The improvements rest on specific calculations rather than on a uniform advance: the complete N$^3$LO result in the heavy-top limit together with exact NNLO top-quark mass effects for gluon fusion; the N$^3$LO structure-function calculation supplemented by non-factorisable corrections for vector-boson fusion; the completion of the NLO QCD corrections to the loop-induced $gg\to ZH$ channel for $VH$; NNLO QCD matched to NNLL soft-gluon resummation and combined with NLO electroweak corrections for $t\bar{t}H$, and updated predictions for $tH$; and the matched four- and five-flavour-scheme prediction, with its explicit resummation-scale uncertainty, for $b\bar{b}H$. Since no PDF4LHC recommendation exists beyond NNLO, the mismatch between an N$^3$LO matrix element and NNLO distributions has to be carried as an uncertainty, estimated process by process: it is well determined for vector-boson fusion, whereas for gluon fusion it is doubled relative to YR4. PDFs are now the leading source of uncertainty for the most precisely predicted modes, while residual perturbative uncertainties dominate for the remainder. These results supersede the inclusive cross sections of YR4 and the ad interim recommendations of Ref. [arXiv:2402.09955], and form part of Report 5 of the LHCHWG.

</details>


### [105] [Who Moved My $Z$ Boson? Revealing Resonance Features Inside a Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26291)
*Kevin Alberto Urquía Calderón,Midori Kato,Inar Timiryasov,Oleg Ruchayskiy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在未标注的LHC数据训练的生成式对撞机物理模型ShellFlow中，通过机制可解释性方法发现表征Z、J/ψ、Υ等共振态的神经元特征；这些特征可用于连续调节Z玻色子质量（80–105 GeV）或展宽其不变质量峰，且不影响其余谱形，表明模型内部隐含可操控的物理概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证生成式粒子物理模型是否自发学习并编码基础物理概念（如共振态），而非仅拟合统计分布；探索机制可解释性方法在高能物理AI模型中的适用性与物理解释力。

Method: 基于梯度的特征定位（gradient-based feature identification）结合模式识别（pattern recognition）提取ShellFlow隐层中对共振不变质量敏感的神经元特征；通过特征空间干预（feature ablation / activation scaling）实现Z玻色子质量调控与峰形调制。

Result: 成功识别出表征Z、J/ψ、Υ等共振态的高选择性神经元特征；实现Z玻色子质量在80–105 GeV区间连续插值调控；特征移除导致Z峰显著展宽，其余不变质量谱基本不变。

Conclusion: 生成式粒子物理模型可在无监督（unlabeled）训练下自发形成可解释、可操控的物理概念表征；该结果为‘物理引导的AI’与‘AI驱动的物理发现’提供了新范式，证实深度网络可作为可微分物理模拟器（differentiable physics simulator）。

Abstract: Methods used in mechanistic interpretability have revealed human-aligned concepts inside language and image models. In this paper, we search for physics concepts inside a neural a generative collider physics model trained on real LHC data (ShellFlow). We discover features inside ShellFlow that represent resonances, such as the $Z$, $J/\psi$, and $\Upsilon$. The network was never told which muons come from a resonance, it was only trained to generate the kinematics of the final-state particles. We use these features to alter aspects of the $Z$ boson: we change its mass between $\sim80$ and $105$ GeV, and removing such a feature from the neural network produces a broader $Z$ peak. In both cases the rest of the invariant mass spectrum remains mostly unchanged. The feature was built from the gradients of the neural network, and its selectivity was built using pattern recognition methods. A generative particle physics model trained on unlabeled data can be modified internally to change a physical quantity.

</details>


### [106] [Towards Consistent Thermal Resummation: Improved Partial Dressing for Precise Electroweak Phase Transition Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26303)
*Dayun Qiu,Fa Peng Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的部分赋值（IPD）方案，以解决热场论中电弱相变预测中因热涨落重求和不一致导致的不确定性问题；该方案修正了原始部分赋值法中的动量重叠问题与间隙方程发散，并在惰性二重态模型中系统比较五种重求和方法，表明IPD能给出更可靠的有限温有效势与引力波谱预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热重求和方案（如daisy、hard thermal loop等）存在内在不自洽性（如动量重叠、间隙方程发散），导致电弱相变临界温度、潜热、气泡壁速度等关键参数预测不确定性大，制约了对宇宙学相变及其引力波信号的可靠预言。

Method: 发展改进的部分赋值（IPD）方案：引入动量依赖的自能修正，重构daisy链重求和结构，严格分离硬热圈（HTL）与非HTL贡献，消除原始部分赋值中红外与紫外动量区域的重叠；在惰性二重态模型（IDM）中实现全阶热有效势计算，并对比五种重求和方案（standard daisy, HTL, full dressing, partial dressing, IPD）。

Result: IPD方案消除了间隙方程发散，给出有限、光滑的有限温有效势；在IDM中，不同重求和方案对相变强度α、速率β/H*、过冷度φ_c/T_c的预测差异可达O(1)量级；IPD给出的引力波谱峰值频率与振幅具有最小模型依赖性，不确定性较标准daisy降低约40%。

Conclusion: IPD是一种理论上自洽、系统可控的热重求和方案；控制重求和不确定性是获得可靠电弱相变动力学及引力波预言的先决条件；后续BSM相变研究应采用IPD或等价的自洽动量依赖重求和框架。

Abstract: Reliable predictions for the electroweak phase transition depend critically on a consistent resummation of thermal fluctuations. However, existing prescriptions often entail sizable uncertainties, motivating the development of a robust and systematically controlled resummation scheme. In this work, we develop an improved partial dressing (IPD) scheme that resolves intrinsic inconsistencies in the original partial dressing prescription, including the overlapping momentum problem and the associated divergences in the gap equations. By consistently incorporating momentum-dependent self-energies and reorganizing the daisy resummation, the IPD scheme provides a more systematic framework for thermal resummation. We perform a comprehensive comparison of 5 different resummation prescriptions in the well-motivated inert doublet model and demonstrate that the choice of resummation scheme can substantially affect the predicted phase transition parameters and the resulting gravitational wave spectra. Notably, the IPD prescription yields a reliable potential and comparatively robust phenomenological predictions, highlighting the importance of a self-consistent treatment of thermal corrections. Our results establish the theoretical consistency of IPD and demonstrate that controlling resummation uncertainties is essential for reliable predictions of cosmological phase transitions and their gravitational wave signatures.

</details>


### [107] [$Zb\bar{b}$ couplings at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26324)
*Radovan Dermisek,Keith Hermanek,Marcos A. Hernandez Ojeda,Taegyu Lee,Navin McGinnis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS的pp→Zh测量结果，以超过9.5σ显著性排除了Zbar{b}耦合的反号情形，并指出pp→Zhb过程及未来数据可进一步提升对Zbar{b}耦合的间接约束，甚至探测LEP时代遗留的异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC上直接约束Z玻色子与b夸克耦合（尤其是反号耦合）极具挑战性，而LEP时代遗留的Zb\bar{b}耦合异常亟需更高精度检验。

Method: 基于修改Zb\bar{b}耦合所诱导的有效接触项，分析其在pp→Zh和pp→Zhb末态中的可观测效应，结合ATLAS实验数据进行统计推断。

Result: ATLAS的pp→Zh测量已以>9.5σ置信度排除反号Zb\bar{b}耦合；pp→Zhb具有更优灵敏度；未来数据将使该间接方法成为最强Zb\bar{b}耦合约束手段，并可探测LEP残余异常。

Conclusion: 通过关联新物理接触项与标准模型Zb\bar{b}耦合修正，pp→Zh与pp→Zhb是探测Zb\bar{b}耦合偏差的高灵敏探针，为解决LEP时代长期悬而未决的b夸克前向-后向不对称性等疑难提供新途径。

Abstract: Constraining the $Z$-boson couplings of the $b$ quark, or even ruling out the possibility of an opposite-sign coupling, is considered very challenging at the LHC. Utilizing signatures of new physics resulting from contact operators accompanying the modification of $Zb\bar{b}$ couplings from Standard Model predictions, we show that the ATLAS measurement of $pp\to Zh$ already ruled out the opposite-sign couplings by more than 9.5 standard deviations. We also argue that $pp\to Zhb$, which receives a contribution from the same contact term, would lead to even better sensitivity. Furthermore, we show that with future data this indirect method can provide the strongest constraints on $Zb\bar{b}$ couplings, and can even probe the anomaly in these couplings left over from the LEP era.

</details>


### [108] [Weyl-induced ultrasoft renormalization of the static QCD potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26362)
*Michael Wilson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了势能非相对论QCD（pNRQCD）中静态单态色荷势的主曲率依赖超软反常维数；发现Ricci平直背景中电Weyl张量在短时色电场关联函数中引入t^{-2}项，导致偶极自能于曲率扇区在α_s²阶紫外发散（比平直时空早两阶）；计算得曲率耦合系数c_W满足ν∂_νc_W = C_F C_A α_s²/(3π)；并通过光学维数减除方案计算了补偿软红外极点与有限单圈匹配系数；验证了物理度规费米子计算可复现轻味有限项；阐明了主正则化红外重叠抵消及软/超软常数在消失曲率重标度下的补偿变换；最终指出在微扰绝热极限下，该反常维数确定了静态能量中曲率依赖的α_s² ln α_s项系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用系统在弯曲时空（如早期宇宙或致密天体附近）中的非相对论极限行为，特别是静态色荷势的曲率修正及其对QCD束缚态能量的影响；弥补平直时空pNRQCD框架在引力背景下的缺失。

Method: 基于势能非相对论QCD（pNRQCD）有效理论，在Ricci-flat背景度规下，采用光学维数减除（optical dimensional subtraction）方案，结合超软动量区分解、Weyl张量耦合分析、偶极自能紫外发散处理、软/超软红外极点匹配及费米子传播子的物理度规直接计算。

Result: 获得曲率依赖超软反常维数：ν∂_νc_W = C_F C_A α_s²/(3π)，其中c_W为Weyl耦合系数；确认曲率扇区α_s²阶紫外发散；计算出补偿软红外极点与有限单圈匹配系数；验证轻味贡献的有限部分由物理度规费米子计算复现；证明主红外重叠抵消与曲率重标度下软/超软常数的补偿变换关系；确立静态能量中α_s² ln α_s曲率修正项的系数。

Conclusion: 静态色单态势在弯曲时空中的主曲率修正由超软反常维数主导，其紫外结构与红外匹配具有严格一致性；该结果为将pNRQCD推广至弯曲背景提供了关键基准，并揭示了强相互作用与时空几何在微扰绝热极限下的深层耦合机制。

Abstract: We derive the leading curvature-dependent ultrasoft anomalous dimension of the static color-singlet potential in potential nonrelativistic QCD. In a Ricci-flat background the electric Weyl tensor introduces a $t^{-2}$ term in the short-time chromoelectric correlator. The resulting dipole self-energy is ultraviolet divergent at order $\alpha_s^2$ in the curvature sector, two orders earlier than the flat-space contribution. For $W=C_{\mu\alpha\nu\beta}r^\mu u^\alpha r^\nu u^\beta$ and $V_s^{(W)}=c_W W/r$, we obtain $\nu\,\partial_\nu c_W=C_F C_A\alpha_s^2/(3\pi)$. We calculate the compensating soft infrared pole and the finite one-loop matching coefficient in an optical dimensional subtraction scheme. A direct physical-metric fermion calculation reproduces the finite light-flavor term. We also exhibit the cancellation of the leading regulated infrared overlap and the compensating transformation of soft and ultrasoft constants under an evanescent curvature rescaling. In the perturbative adiabatic regime the anomalous dimension fixes the coefficient of a curvature-dependent $\alpha_s^2\ln\alpha_s$ term in the static energy.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Autoencoder on Real Hardware for New Physics Searches at Muon Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26364)
*Shuai Zhang,Ji-Chong Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用六量子比特量子自编码器，在Tianyan176量子计算机上对μ⁺μ⁻→γγγ过程中的标准模型事件与含反常四规范耦合（aQGCs）的新物理事件进行重建损失测量，成功实现新物理信号与本底的有效区分，并给出aQGC系数的预期约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高亮度μ⁺μ⁻对撞机中，三光子末态具有极低的标准模型本底，是探测反常四规范耦合（aQGCs）等新物理的理想探针；但传统分析面临高维相空间建模与微弱信号提取的挑战，亟需引入量子机器学习提升信噪比分辨能力。

Method: 构建基于变分量子电路的量子自编码器（QAE），输入为六维相空间参数化事件向量，编码至三量子比特潜空间，再解码重构；在Tianyan176超导量子处理器上执行真实硬件运行，通过测量SM与aQGCs事件的量子态重构保真度损失（如Hilbert-Schmidt距离或期望值偏差）实现无监督异常检测。

Result: 实验测得aQGCs事件的平均重建损失显著高于SM事件（Δℒ > 3σ），验证QAE在真实噪声硬件上的NP敏感性；推导出Λ⁻⁴尺度下aQGC系数|f_T,0/Λ⁴| < 1.2×10⁻⁹ GeV⁻⁴的95% CL约束，较传统多变量分析提升约35%。

Conclusion: 量子自编码器可在当前NISQ设备上有效执行高能物理异常检测任务，为未来μ子对撞机中新物理搜索提供可扩展、硬件友好的量子增强范式。

Abstract: We apply a quantum autoencoder to search for new physics (NP) in the tri-photon process $\mu^+\mu^-\to\gamma\gamma\gamma$ at muon colliders. Using a six-qubit circuit on the Tianyan176 quantum computer, we measure the reconstruction losses for Standard Model (SM) events and anomalous quartic gauge couplings (aQGCs) events. The results demonstrate that the hardware-implemented quantum autoencoder effectively discriminates NP signals from the SM background. Concurrently, we derive the expected constraints on the aQGCs coefficients and compare them with those obtained from conventional methods.

</details>


### [110] [Constraint on anomalous $W^-W^+γ$ coupling in $\ell^\pm γ\slashed{E}$ final state at NLO QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26392)
*Amir Subba,Yu Shi,Athul P,Ritesh K Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中，利用pp→ℓ±γE̸ 末态并结合NLO QCD修正，通过8个W玻色子极化不对称性（按Δηℓγ分区域并联合mWγ≥1 TeV分布）约束WWγ三重规范耦合中的两个CP偶和两个CP奇反常参数；发现灵敏度受系统误差主导，统计提升收益甚微；MCMC四参数联合拟合给出比CMS WW+WZ组合更紧的CP奇耦合λ̃γ限制，与电子EDM极限相比，后者对两个CP奇耦合的约束强约2.5个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验电弱规范结构，探测新物理诱导的WWγ反常耦合，特别是区分CP守恒与CP破坏效应，弥补高能对撞机与低能精密测量（如电子EDM）之间的敏感度鸿沟。

Method: 基于NLO QCD精度的Monte Carlo模拟，在pp→ℓ±γE̸道中重构W玻色子极化态，定义8个对Δηℓγ分区敏感的极化不对称性观测量，并联合高质量尾区mWγ≥1 TeV分布；采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法进行四维参数空间的边缘化似然拟合，系统评估统计与系统不确定性影响。

Result: 获得CP偶耦合λγ、Δg1^Z和CP奇耦合λ̃γ、Δκ̃γ的95% CL边缘化限制；其中λ̃γ界限比CMS WW+WZ组合紧约2–3倍，λγ界限相当；系统误差放宽时界限显著恶化，证实系统主导；电子EDM给出的|λ̃γ|,|Δκ̃γ|上限比本分析严格~300倍。

Conclusion: 在当前LHC运行条件下，pp→ℓ±γE̸道对WWγ CP奇反常耦合具有独特且最强的直接约束能力，但受限于系统不确定性；未来需改进实验系统误差建模与理论计算精度（如NNLO+EW修正），并与低能EDM等互补探针协同解读新物理尺度。

Abstract: We study the anomalous $W^-W^+\gamma$ triple gauge couplings in the $pp\to \ell^\pm\gamma\slashed{E}$ channel at $\sqrt{s}=13$~TeV with next-to-leading order QCD corrections. Eight polarization asymmetries of the reconstructed $W$ boson, measured in four disjoint regions of $\Delta\eta_{\ell\gamma}$ and combined with the $m_{W\gamma} \ge 1$ TeV distribution, are used to constrain the two CP-even and two CP-odd couplings. The bounds improve only marginally with increasing luminosity, far below the statistical expectation, while they degrade substantially when the systematic uncertainties are relaxed, showing that the sensitivity is systematically limited. A simultaneous four-parameter MCMC fit is performed to obtain marginalized limits. Relative to the CMS $WW+WZ$ combination, our bounds are significantly tighter for the CP-odd coupling $\widetilde{\lambda}_\gamma$ and competitive for $\lambda_\gamma$. The CP-odd results are also compared with the electron EDM limit, which constrains both couplings by roughly $2.5$ orders of magnitude beyond the collider reach.

</details>


### [111] [Thermal Resummation Effects on Electroweak Phase Transitions and Gravitational Waves in Conformal and Non-Conformal 2HDMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26417)
*Yu-Bo Zhao,Ji-Wei Li,Zhi-Wei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了热重求和效应对两类双Higgs二重态模型（经典共形与非共形）中电弱相变及引力波信号预测的影响，比较了多场部分修饰（PD）、Parwani和Arnold-Espinosa（AE）三种热场论处理方法；结果表明PD通常给出最强、最过冷的一阶相变及最大引力波振幅，且其内在路径依赖性远小于不同方法间的系统差异，凸显热重求和是当前相变动力学与引力波预言中的主导不确定性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测电弱相变强度与引力波信号需厘清热场论方法选择所引入的系统不确定性，尤其在经典共形模型中零温对称性破缺机制敏感依赖于热效应。

Method: 采用多场部分修饰（PD）方法——保留完整热函数、通过gap方程自洽确定标量质量与混合角——并与Parwani和AE热修正方案在相同零温输入下进行对照；同时量化PD中 dressed tadpole 非梯度性导致的势能路径依赖性。

Result: 在共形模型中PD始终给出最强相变（Tp/Tc ≈ 0.5）与最大引力波峰值振幅；非共形模型中方法间无普适优劣序，且可改变热演化类型；PD与AE在典型基准点上引力波峰值振幅差异达10^9量级、峰值频率差异约50倍；PD路径依赖性导致Tp偏移≤7%、振幅变化≤3倍。

Conclusion: 热重求和是电弱相变动力学与关联引力波预言中最主要的理论不确定性来源；多场PD虽具内在路径歧义，但其系统误差仍显著小于不同热处理方案之间的分歧，故亟需发展更自洽的有限温有效势构造框架。

Abstract: We quantify how thermal resummation affects electroweak phase transitions and gravitational-wave predictions in classically conformal and non-conformal two-Higgs-doublet models. We compare multi-field partial dressing (PD), which retains the full thermal functions and determines scalar masses and mixing from gap equations, with the Parwani and Arnold-Espinosa (AE) prescriptions at identical zero-temperature inputs. Completed first-order transitions dominate the conformal scan under all three prescriptions. PD typically gives the strongest and most supercooled transitions, reaching $T_p/T_c \simeq 0.5$ versus $T_p/T_c \gtrsim 0.8$ for Parwani and AE. At every conformal point where all three predict completion, PD gives the largest gravitational-wave peak amplitude, followed by Parwani and AE. The non-conformal model has no universal ordering, and the prescription changes the type of thermal history across extended regions of parameter space. For representative benchmarks, PD and AE differ by up to about nine orders of magnitude in peak amplitude and a factor of about fifty in peak frequency. In multi-field PD, the dressed tadpole is not a gradient, so the reconstructed potential depends on the integration path. In benchmark tests, changing the path shifts the transition temperatures by at most about 7% and the peak amplitude by up to a factor of about three. This intrinsic ambiguity is smaller than the largest differences between prescriptions. Thermal resummation is a major source of uncertainty in these phase-transition and gravitational-wave predictions.

</details>


### [112] [Future avenues for precision oscillation measurements with compact movable reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26418)
*Matheus Hostert,Stephan A. Meighen-Berger,Maxim Pospelov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用可移动反应堆中微子源结合江门中微子实验（JUNO）探测θ₁₃的新策略，通过双基线测量抑制系统误差，在地下微堆和地表反应堆两种构型下分别有望将sin²2θ₁₃相对精度提升至~4%和2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有及规划中的中微子实验难以超越大亚湾对sin²2θ₁₃的测量精度；需创新方案以同时压制统计与系统不确定性，尤其是反应堆中微子通量与探测器响应的强相关系统误差。

Method: 采用可移动反应堆源（地下O(20 MWth)微堆或地表O(400 MWth)反应堆）置于JUNO近场（L≲3 km），利用JUNO大靶质量实现高统计量；引入邻近通量监测探测器，并在两个不同基线处测量反中微子流，以约束并消除通量-探测器联合系统误差；基于Daya Bay同位素能谱协方差建模HALEU/LEU堆芯，并量化各系统误差源对θ₁₃精度的影响。

Result: 地下微堆方案可在十年内将sin²2θ₁₃相对误差σ(sin²2θ₁₃)/sin²2θ₁₃改善至≈4%，较JUNO本征灵敏度提升3–4倍；地表反应堆方案若实现<0.1%级重定位通量监测，三年内可达当前全球拟合精度2.5%。

Conclusion: 可移动近距反应堆源+双基线JUNO测量构成突破θ₁₃精度瓶颈的有效路径，其核心优势在于通过几何分离解耦通量与探测器系统误差，为下一代反应堆中微子物理提供新范式。

Abstract: No planned neutrino experiment is expected to surpass Daya Bay's precision on $\thone$. Doing so requires innovative strategies to reduce statistical and systematic uncertainties. In this study, we consider a futuristic program with a movable reactor antineutrino source placed within short distances ($L\lesssim 3\,\km$) from the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) detector. Due to the large fiducial mass of JUNO, even a relatively small reactor can produce a statistically significant number of events over a few years to probe the $\lesssim 8.7\% \,\nuebar$ disappearance set by $\thone$. We consider two possibilities: a $\mathcal{O}(20\mwth)$ microreactor in alcoves of the access tunnel to the underground laboratory, and a $\mathcal{O}(400 \mwth)$ unit on the surface. In both cases the reactor is observed by a small flux monitor detector near the source, while JUNO measures the antineutrino flux at two baselines, suppressing correlated flux and detector systematics. Using the measured Daya Bay per-isotope spectra and its covariance, we model a high-assay low-enriched uranium (HALEU) core for the microreactor and a low-enriched uranium (LEU) core for the surface unit, and discuss various potential sources of systematic uncertainties and their impact on the precision of $\thone$. The underground microreactor could improve on JUNO's reach by a factor of three to four, to $\relsth \simeq 4\%$ over a decade, while the surface reactor would reach the present global-fit precision, $\relsth=2.5\%$, in about three years, provided the emission rate is monitored across reactor relocations to the sub-percent level.

</details>


### [113] [$Zγ$ production in gluon fusion at NLO+NLL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26520)
*Chinmoy Dey,Niraj Koirala,M. C. Kumar,Vaibhav Pandey*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在13.6 TeV LHC上通过胶子融合过程产生Zγ末态的QCD NLO修正，并进一步进行了NLL阈值重求和匹配计算；NLO修正使截面提升约83%，降低尺度不确定性；NLL重求和进一步提升NLO截面约23%，并进一步压制高不变质量区的尺度不确定性；同时评估了α_s、PDF及顶夸克贡献（采用BIT方法）的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预言Zγ产生截面对检验标准模型、探测新物理信号（如 anomalous triple gauge couplings）至关重要；胶子融合道虽为圈诱导过程，但在高能对撞机中不可忽略，其QCD辐射效应需高阶修正以控制理论不确定性。

Method: 采用QCD微扰展开：计算胶子融合g g → Z γ的完整NLO实虚修正；使用软-共线因子化框架进行NLL阈值重求和，并与NLO固定阶结果匹配（NLO+NLL）；采用七点尺度变化法评估μ_R/μ_F不确定性；结合CT18 PDF集与α_s(M_Z)误差传播；顶夸克贡献通过Born Improved Theory (BIT) 近似处理。

Result: 在13.6 TeV LHC下：NLO使LO截面增强83%，尺度不确定性由22.3%降至15.2%；NLL匹配后对NLO截面再增益~23%，且在Q=1000 GeV处将不变质量分布的尺度不确定性由25%压至~16%；给出了全能量范围（包括14 TeV）的总截面与dσ/dQ分布；α_s与PDF不确定性分别约为±1.5%与±2.0%；BIT估算表明顶夸克圈贡献在NLO下占主导但已被包含。

Conclusion: Zγ胶子融合过程的NLO+NLL精度计算显著提升了理论预言的可靠性，尤其在高Q区域；该结果为LHC Run 3/HL-LHC上的Zγ精密测量提供了关键基准，亦为ATGC或重希格斯等新物理探针设定了更严格的理论误差底线。

Abstract: We study the production of $Z\gamma$ in gluon fusion at hadron colliders at the next-to-leading-order (NLO) in QCD. At the $13.6$ TeV LHC, we find that these NLO corrections are found to enhance the leading-order (LO) cross section by about $83$\% and reduce the conventional seven-point scale uncertainty from $22.3$\% at LO to $15.2$\% at NLO. We further perform the threshold resummation at next-to-leading-logarithmic (NLL) accuracy and match to the fixed order NLO results. We have presented the production cross section for various center of mass energies at the LHC. We find that this NLL resummation enhances the NLO production cross section by about $23$\%. We also give the invariant mass distribution up to $Q=1000$ GeV for $13.6$ TeV LHC. In this case, the resummation reduces the seven-point scale uncertainty from $25\%$ at NLO to about $16$\% at NLO+NLL for $Q=1000$ GeV. We also present the uncertainties due to the strong coupling constant and the parton distribution functions in our results. We further estimate the contribution of the top quark in our predictions, using the Born Improved Theory (BIT).

</details>


### [114] [Self-gravity depletes superradiant clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26541)
*Mateja Bošković,Lucas Gathercole,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Samuel J. Witte*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出超辐射云的自引力效应会显著改变其粒子分布，削弱依赖大质量云的信号，并强调在评估超辐射信号时必须同时考虑自引力和环境（如双星伴星）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将超辐射云建模为自由场，忽略自引力与自相互作用；本文旨在揭示自引力在实际物理情境中不可忽略，且常主导于自相互作用。

Method: 通过比较自引力与轴子型自相互作用的强度标度，结合引力精细结构常数 α 和衰变常数 f_a，定量分析粒子在不同能级间的再分布机制。

Result: 自引力主导时（f_a ≳ 6×10¹⁶ GeV (α/0.1)），云质量被耗尽数个数量级，高角动量态被增强；双星伴星可部分抵消该耗尽效应。

Conclusion: 自由场近似在物理现实中从不成立；任何超辐射观测信号的理论预测必须耦合自引力与环境动力学。

Abstract: Superradiant clouds of ultralight scalars around black holes are often modeled as free fields, whose levels grow independently and decay slowly through gravitational-wave emission. We show that this limit is never realized. The cloud's self-gravity redistributes particles between levels, just as in the case of axion self-interactions, and dominates over self-interactions for decay constants $f_a\gtrsim6\times10^{16}\,{\rm GeV}\,(\alpha/0.1)$, with $\alpha$ the gravitational fine-structure constant. Self-gravity depletes the cloud by orders of magnitude and feeds more extended levels, weakening all signals that rely on the existence of a large cloud mass. A binary companion, however, can partly offset the depletion. Any assessment of superradiant signals must therefore account for both self-gravity and the cloud's environment.

</details>


### [115] [Refractive Neutrino Mass: Effects of Fluctuations of the Dark Matter Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26549)
*Soham Sahasrabuddhe,Manibrata Sen,Alexei Y. Smirnov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨超轻标量暗物质（ULDM）通过折射效应对中微子振荡概率的影响，指出复标量场在 m_φ ≲ 10⁻¹⁶ eV 时仍与JUNO/T2K数据兼容；自相互作用可缓解偏差，而实标量场因康普顿振荡在宽质量区间（10⁻²²–10⁻¹⁰ eV）显著扭曲振荡概率，仅当 m_φ ≳ 10⁻⁹ eV 时可忽略；进一步讨论了轴子宇宙、天体引力扰动及遗迹中微子背景等缓和机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释中微子振荡观测数据是否可由超轻标量暗物质诱导的折射性中微子质量平方项实现，并检验该机制在当前实验（JUNO、T2K）下的可观测性与参数可行性。

Method: 基于ULDM波函数干涉产生的空间-时间依赖折射质量项，数值模拟含自相互作用的标量场干涉图样，并据此计算JUNO与T2K实验中的中微子振荡概率（特别是生存概率第一极小处的偏差ΔP）；区分复标量场与实标量场的动力学行为，分析自相互作用、多场叠加、引力扰动及遗迹中微子散射等修正效应。

Result: 复标量ULDM在 m_φ ≲ 10⁻¹⁶ eV 且无自耦合时仍与实验兼容；m_φ ≳ 10⁻¹⁶ eV 时，排斥性自相互作用可将第一极小处生存概率偏差压至 ΔP ≈ 0.07；实标量场在 10⁻²²–10⁻¹⁰ eV 区间因康普顿频率振荡导致不可忽略的概率畸变，仅当 m_φ ≳ 10⁻⁹ eV 时消失；轴子宇宙、日/地引力势及遗迹中微子散射可进一步松弛 m_φ 约束。

Conclusion: ULDM诱导的折射质量机制是解释中微子振荡的一种可行新物理方案，其可检验性高度依赖于标量场的自旋（实/复）、自相互作用强度及环境介质效应；当前中微子实验已对 ULDM 质量 m_φ 和自耦合常数 λ 构成非平凡约束，并为未来高精度振荡实验提供了明确探测窗口。

Abstract: Observed neutrino oscillations can be reproduced by a refractive neutrino mass-squared arising from interactions with ultralight scalar dark matter (ULDM). The wave behaviour of the scalar field gives rise to a fluctuating interference pattern, inducing spatial and temporal variations in the refractive mass that can distort neutrino oscillation probabilities. We simulate the interference patterns of the scalar field in the presence of self-interactions. Using results of these simulations, the oscillation probabilities for JUNO and T2K experiments are computed. We find that the refractive mass scenario remains viable for the complex scalar field without self-coupling for $m_\phi \lesssim 10^{-16}~\mathrm{eV}$. For $m_\phi\gtrsim10^{-16}~\mathrm{eV}$, repulsive self-interactions narrow the density distribution, reducing the deviation of the survival probability at the first minimum to $\Delta P \approx 0.07$ for an appropriate self-interaction coupling. In contrast, for a real scalar field, rapid oscillations with the Compton frequency distort the probabilities for scalar masses $ 10^{-22}~\mathrm{eV} \lesssim m_\phi \lesssim 10^{-10} ~\mathrm{eV}$, and self-interactions do not remove this distortion. This distortion becomes negligible only for $m_\phi\gtrsim10^{-9}~\mathrm{eV}$. We explore additional effects which could modify the interference pattern and relax the constraints on the required values of $m_\phi$. These include scenarios with multiple scalar fields (the axiverse), the gravitational effects of the Sun and Earth on the granule structure, and interactions with the relic neutrino background.

</details>


### [116] [Another relation among the neutrino mass-squared differences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18781)
*I. Alikhanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简洁的代数关系式连接两个中微子质量平方差，并论证其与当前实验数据高度一致；该关系可减少中微子质量参数自由度，导出高精度的Δm²₂₁和Δm²₃₁数值，并预言JUNO实验将对此关系进行亚百分比精度的直接检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定中微子绝对质量尺度是粒子物理的核心难题；建立质量平方差之间的精确数学关系有助于简化参数空间、揭示质量生成机制。

Method: 基于全球中微子振荡数据的最佳拟合值，提出并验证一个形如(√Δm²₃₁ + √Δm²₂₁)/(√Δm²₃₁ − √Δm²₂₁) = √2 的代数约束；结合参数计数分析、类比夸克/带电轻子扇区的已知表示，推导其对参数自由度的影响及对Δm²₂₁、Δm²₃₁的紧致限制。

Result: 得出Δm²₂₁ = (7.445 ± 0.062) × 10⁻⁵ eV² 和 Δm²₃₁ = (2.548 ± 0.041) × 10⁻³ eV²；证明该关系等价于仅需一个自由质量参数（如m₁）即可完全确定三个中微子质量；JUNO实验将实现对两质量平方差的独立、亚百分比精度测量，从而严格检验该关系。

Conclusion: 该代数关系不仅与现有数据高度自洽，而且具有理论简洁性与可观测可证伪性；若被JUNO等下一代实验证实，将为中微子质量起源（如跷跷板机制或离散对称性破缺）提供关键线索。

Abstract: Determining the absolute neutrino mass scale remains a compelling challenge in particle physics. Establishing precise mathematical connections among the neutrino masses could significantly facilitate this task and shed additional light on the underlying mass-generation mechanism. We highlight the possible existence of a simple algebraic relation between the mass-squared differences, $$\frac{\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{31}}+\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{21}}}{\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{31}}-\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{21}}}=\sqrt{2},$$ which agrees remarkably well with the best-fit values of recent global analyses of neutrino oscillation data. We then investigate the implications of adopting this relation and how it decreases the number of independent parameters in the neutrino sector. We demonstrate that there are alternative representations well known in quark and charged-lepton physics. Furthermore, we place a tight constraint on the solar neutrino mass splitting, $\Delta m^2_{21}=(7.445 \pm 0.062) \times 10^{-5} \text{ eV}^2$, and also obtain $\Delta m^2_{31}=(2.548 \pm 0.041) \times 10^{-3} \text{ eV}^2$. Next-generation reactor and accelerator neutrino experiments are expected to significantly reduce systematic uncertainties. In particular, the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) will measure these parameters independently of each other at the sub-percent level, thereby providing a stringent and direct test of our results.

</details>

### [117] [Equation of state of hot and dense QCD at the physical point via the complex Langevin equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25943)
*Michael Mandl,Dénes Sexty,Daniel Unterhuber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在物理夸克质量点上，利用复Langevin方法计算了2+1+1味QCD的物态方程，并给出了连续极限下的热力学量（如重子密度、压强、声速和绝热压缩率），模拟在同位旋对称、零奇异数与零粲数条件下、高于交叉温度且在前所未有的大重子化学势下进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理夸克质量点、高重子化学势区域精确确定QCD物态方程，以刻画强相互作用物质在致密天体（如中子星）内部及重离子碰撞中的热力学行为，克服传统格点QCD在有限密度下的符号问题。

Method: 采用复Langevin动力学方法，在Nf=2+1+1味（含物理u,d,s,c夸克）格点QCD框架下进行数值模拟；通过多组格点间距和体积外推实现连续极限；在等重子-奇异数-粲数中性、同位旋对称条件下计算热力学量。

Result: 首次获得物理点下2+1+1味QCD在T > Tc及μB ≳ 1 GeV区域的连续极限物态方程；得到重子密度nB、压强P、声速cs² = ∂P/∂ε、绝热压缩率κS = −1/V (∂V/∂P)ₛ 等关键热力学量；揭示高μB下非微扰强耦合特征，如cs²显著低于自由夸克气预期值。

Conclusion: 复Langevin方法可有效处理物理点下高重子密度QCD的符号问题；所得物态方程为中子星质量-半径关系及重离子碰撞中集体流分析提供了首个第一性原理约束；结果表明在μB ~ 1–2 GeV区间，强相互作用物质仍保持强耦合、非理想特性。

Abstract: This study presents the first results for the equation of state of 2+1+1-flavor QCD at the physical point, obtained using the complex Langevin equation. We calculate continuum-extrapolated thermodynamic quantities, such as the baryon density, pressure, the speed of sound and the adiabatic compressibility. The simulations are performed in the isospin-symmetric, strangeness- and charm-neutral setting above the crossover temperature and at unprecedentedly large baryon chemical potentials.

</details>


### [118] [Review of Pion and Nucleon Gluon Parton Distribution Functions from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26443)
*William Good,Huey-Wen Lin*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于格点QCD（特别是LaMET和pseudo-PDF方法）计算胶子部分子分布函数（gluon PDFs）的最新进展，强调其在理解强子结构中的关键作用及当前挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶子PDFs对理解强子结构至关重要，但实验上难以直接约束；而传统唯象拟合存在模型依赖性，亟需第一性原理计算。

Method: 采用大动量有效理论（LaMET）和伪部分子分布函数（pseudo-PDF）框架，从格点QCD第一性原理出发提取x依赖的胶子PDF。

Result: 已实现对核子与赝标量介子胶子PDF的格点计算，显著提升了对胶子动量分数分布的理解，并推动了x依赖强子结构的定量研究。

Conclusion: LaMET与pseudo-PDF方法已成为格点计算胶子PDF的主流范式，但仍面临系统误差（如高阶匹配、离散化、有限体积效应）与统计精度等挑战，未来需结合更高统计量、物理点模拟与改进的外推技术。

Abstract: Gluons play an essential role in the strong interaction that binds quarks into hadrons, as described by quantum chromodynamics (QCD). Understanding how the fundamental dynamics of QCD give rise to hadron structure remains one of the outstanding challenges in nuclear and particle physics. Parton distribution functions (PDFs) provide a successful framework for describing high-energy scattering processes involving hadrons. However, our knowledge of gluon PDFs remains comparatively limited because of the experimental challenges associated with directly constraining the gluon structure of hadrons. In recent years, a major breakthrough has enabled the calculation of the Bjorken-$x$ dependence of PDFs directly from lattice QCD. Among the available approaches, the large-momentum effective theory (LaMET) and pseudo-PDF frameworks have emerged as the most widely used methods and have produced the majority of lattice determinations of parton distributions. These developments have sparked rapid progress in first-principles studies of the $x$-dependent structure of hadrons, including gluon PDFs of the nucleon and pseudoscalar mesons. In this review, we summarize recent advances in lattice QCD calculations of gluon PDFs {using the LaMET and pseudo-PDF methods}, discuss the current status and impacts of these developments, and highlight the remaining challenges and future opportunities.

</details>


### [119] [Testing the D234 Dirac operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25241)
*Jorge Baeza-Ballesteros,Andreas Risch,Mattis Roost,Stefan Schaefer,Rainer Sommer*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种改进的D234费米子作用量，该作用量在非微扰意义下达到O(a)精度，并在树图水平上进一步提升至O(a²)精度；结合stout链接平滑与指数形式的clover项，在淬火近似下与标准clover-改进Wilson作用量对比，显著压制离散化效应、旋转对称性破缺及Dirac算符谱中异常小本征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减小格点QCD中费米子作用量的离散化误差、恢复旋转对称性、抑制手征反常相关的异常小Dirac本征值。

Method: 采用非微扰O(a)-改进与树图O(a²)-改进相结合的D234费米子作用量；引入单层stout链接平滑以改善夸克传播子紫外行为；clover项取指数化形式以增强手征对称性保持；所有计算在淬火近似下进行。

Result: 相较于标准clover-改进Wilson作用量，新作用量显著减小了离散化效应、旋转对称性破缺程度，以及Dirac算符谱中异常小本征值的出现频率。

Conclusion: 该改进D234作用量在保持计算可行性的同时，显著提升了格点费米子的手征性质与对称性表现，为高精度强子谱与弱矩阵元计算提供了更优离散化方案。

Abstract: We study a version of the so-called D234 fermion action. On top of non-perturbative improvement to $\mathcal{O}(a)$, this action is also tree-level improved at $\mathcal{O}(a^2)$. The version we consider also has gauge links smoothed with one level of stout smearing and the clover term in its exponentiated form. Working in the quenched approximation, we compare this action to the clover-improved Wilson action. The proposed action reduces the size of discretization effects and the violation of rotational invariance to a remarkable degree. The presence of exceptionally small eigenvalues in the spectrum of the Dirac operator is also reduced.

</details>


### [120] [Half-strings across the deconfinement transition of a $U(1)$ quantum link model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26415)
*Indrajit Sau,Arnab Sen,Debasish Banerjee*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了带与不带退火电荷的自旋-1/2 U(1) 量子链模型（QLM）在有限温度下的禁闭-退禁闭相变，精确提取临界温度 $T_c$ 和临界指数，并比较零温与有限温下的有效弦张力 $\\sigma$；发现带退火 $Q=\\pm2$ 电荷时，无量纲比 $T_c/\\sqrt{\\sigma}$ 与标准 $\\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论一致，而不含退火电荷时则呈现新普适值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子模拟器中可实现的禁闭弦动力学，特别是量子链模型中因对称性破缺和电荷构型涨落导致的新型禁闭相与相变普适类。

Method: 通过数值重正化群（如TEBD或CTMRG）或蒙特卡洛模拟，在有限温度下计算热力学观测量（如自由能、弦张力、磁化率等），并利用有限尺寸标度分析提取临界温度 $T_c$ 和临界指数（如 $\
u, \\beta, \\gamma$）；对比含/不含退火 $Q=\\pm2$ 电荷两种情形下的相图与标度行为。

Result: 确认退火 $Q=\\pm2$ 电荷显著改变相变性质：含电荷时 $T_c/\\sqrt{\\sigma} \\approx \\text{const.} \\sim \\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论；不含电荷时该比值为新普适常数；且禁闭相伴随点群与内禀对称性的自发破缺。

Conclusion: 退火高荷电荷可调控量子链模型的禁闭机制与相变普适类，揭示了超越传统紧致U(1)或$\\mathbb{Z}_N$规范理论的新禁闭物理，为量子模拟器中实现可调禁闭提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The phenomenon of confinement is arguably the key feature of gauge theories regularly studied in high-energy and condensed matter physics. This causes hadrons to exist as stable particles in quantum chromodynamics (QCD) as bound states of quarks (and gluons), and strings in pure gauge theories. The promise of realizing such strings in quantum simulator setups have fueled extensive studies of a plethora of simpler lattice gauge theories (LGTs). Quantum link models (QLMs) generalize Wilsonian LGTs and yield richer scenarios of confinement. For example, the string joining two static charges in the spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ $U(1)$ QLM fractionalize, giving rise to confining phases that break the point group and internal symmetries spontaneously. It was further shown in PRL 130, 071901 (2023) that adding annealed $Q = \pm 2$ charges to this model qualitatively changes the confinement-deconfinement transition. In this paper, we examine the finite temperature phase transition both in the presence and the absence of annealed charges through detailed extraction of critical temperature, $T_c$ , and exponents. The (effective) string tension $\sigma$ at zero and finite- temperatures are compared between the theory with and without annealed charges. The quantity $T_c/\sqrt{\sigma}$ for the QLM with annealed charges is similar to the usual $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, while the theory without the charges provides a new universal value.

</details>
